Perfect! Now I have a complete picture. Let me create a comprehensive report:

## Frontend Codebase Exploration Report

I've thoroughly explored the Changelogue frontend codebase. Here's what I found:

### 1. **Layout Architecture**

**Root Layout** (`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/layout.tsx`):
- Single root layout file for the entire application
- Uses a **flex layout with sidebar + main content area**:
  - `<Sidebar />` on the left (200px wide, dark background)
  - Right side contains: `<Header />` (12px height bar) + `<main>` (scrollable)
- All pages are wrapped by this layout
- **No nested layouts** exist - only this one root layout

**Key files:**
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/layout/sidebar.tsx` - Navigation component
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/layout/header.tsx` - Breadcrumb header component

---

### 2. **Header/Navigation Components**

**Global Header** (`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/layout/header.tsx`):
- Renders a **12px-high breadcrumb header** that displays the current page location
- Dynamically generates breadcrumbs from URL pathname
- Maps route segments to human-readable labels (e.g., "projects" → "Projects")
- Filters out UUIDs from the breadcrumb display
- Single unified header for all pages

**Global Sidebar Navigation** (`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/layout/sidebar.tsx`):
- Dark themed (dark gray background)
- Fixed navigation items: Dashboard, Projects, Releases, Channels, Subscriptions
- Shows active indicator on current section

---

### 3. **Page Structure - Top-Level Pages**

All top-level pages (`/web/app/*/page.tsx`) include **their own page-level headers**:

| Page | Location | Header Type |
|------|----------|------------|
| **Dashboard** | `/web/app/page.tsx` | No explicit header (just content with StatsCards) |
| **Projects** | `/web/app/projects/page.tsx` | ✅ Has `<h1>Projects</h1>` + description + "New Project" button (lines 147-168) |
| **Releases** | `/web/app/releases/page.tsx` | ✅ Has `<h1>Releases</h1>` + project filter dropdown (lines 135-173) |
| **Channels** | `/web/app/channels/page.tsx` | ✅ Has `<h1>Channels</h1>` + "New Channel" button (lines 54-79) |
| **Subscriptions** | `/web/app/subscriptions/page.tsx` | ✅ Has `<h1>Subscriptions</h1>` + "New Subscription" button (lines 56-81) |
| **Sources** | `/web/app/sources/page.tsx` | ✅ Has `<h2>All Sources</h2>` + description (lines 35-39) |

---

### 4. **Header Duplication - Critical Finding**

**There IS header duplication:**

- **Global header** (from root layout): Renders breadcrumb at top of page (e.g., "Projects", "Releases")
- **Page-level headers**: Each page renders its own `<h1>` title section with additional UI (buttons, filters)

**Example from Projects page:**
- Global header shows: `"Projects"` (breadcrumb)
- Page-level section shows: `"Projects"` (h1) + description + "New Project" button

This means users see the page title **twice** - once in the thin breadcrumb bar and once as the main page heading.

---

### 5. **Detail Page Structure**

Dynamic pages render their content through client components:

| Route | Component | Structure |
|-------|-----------|-----------|
| `/projects/[id]` | `ProjectDetail` | Has internal header zone with title, description, action buttons, and tab navigation (lines 183-287) |
| `/releases/[id]` | `ReleaseDetail` | Has back link, title, metadata display, and cards (lines 122-202) |
| `/projects/[id]/semantic-releases/[srId]` | `SemanticReleaseDetail` | (Rendered as client component) |
| `/projects/[id]/edit` | `ProjectEdit` | (Rendered as client component) |

**Key observation:** Detail pages have their own internal headers/titles, NOT relying on the global breadcrumb header.

---

### 6. **Component Organization**

```
/web/components/
├── layout/
│   ├── header.tsx (global breadcrumb)
│   └── sidebar.tsx (global nav)
├── dashboard/
│   ├── stats-cards.tsx
│   ├── recent-releases.tsx
│   └── activity-feed.tsx
├── projects/
│   ├── project-detail.tsx (has internal header + tabs)
│   ├── project-edit.tsx
│   └── project-form.tsx
├── releases/
│   └── release-detail.tsx (has internal header)
├── semantic-releases/
│   └── semantic-release-detail.tsx
└── ui/
    ├── provider-badge.tsx
    ├── version-chip.tsx
    ├── section-label.tsx
    └── [...other UI components]
```

---

### 7. **Summary of Header Issues**

**Duplication Points:**

1. **Main list pages** (Projects, Releases, Channels, Subscriptions):
   - Global breadcrumb header: Shows "Projects", "Releases", etc.
   - Page-level h1: Duplicates the title again with styling
   - **Result:** Title appears twice on the page

2. **Detail pages** (ProjectDetail, ReleaseDetail):
   - Global breadcrumb: Tries to show path like "Projects / [uuid]" (UUID filtered out, just shows "Projects")
   - Page-level detail header: Shows actual title inside the component
   - **Result:** Better separation since page-level headers are more specific

**No nested layouts found** - all pages share the single root layout.

---

### 8. **Design Observations**

- **Breadcrumb header is minimal**: 12px height, just text
- **Page-level headers are rich**: Include descriptions, action buttons, filters
- **Detail components manage their own layout**: They extend beyond the main content area with negative margins (e.g., `-m-6 mb-0` in ProjectDetail)
- **Consistent styling**: Uses Fraunces and DM Sans fonts, orange accent color (#e8601a)

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
     4→
     5→const SEGMENT_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
     6→  "": "Dashboard",
     7→  projects: "Projects",
     8→  releases: "Releases",
     9→  sources: "Sources",
    10→  subscriptions: "Subscriptions",
    11→  channels: "Channels",
    12→  agent: "Agent",
    13→  "semantic-releases": "Semantic Releases",
    14→  "context-sources": "Context Sources",
    15→  new: "New",
    16→  edit: "Edit",
    17→};
    18→
    19→function segmentLabel(seg: string): string {
    20→  return SEGMENT_LABELS[seg] ?? seg;
    21→}
    22→
    23→export function Header() {
    24→  const pathname = usePathname();
    25→  const segments = pathname.split("/").filter(Boolean);
    26→
    27→  const uuidRe = /^[0-9a-f-]{8,}$/i;
    28→  const breadcrumbs = segments
    29→    .filter((s) => !uuidRe.test(s))
    30→    .map(segmentLabel);
    31→
    32→  const display =
    33→    breadcrumbs.length === 0
    34→      ? "Dashboard"
    35→      : breadcrumbs.join(" / ");
    36→
    37→  return (
    38→    <header
    39→      className="flex h-12 items-center px-6"
    40→      style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
    41→    >
    42→      <p
    43→        className="text-[14px] font-medium text-[#111113]"
    44→        style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
    45→      >
    46→        {display}
    47→      </p>
    48→    </header>
    49→  );
    50→}
    51→

    26→  const { data: allSRs, isLoading } = useSWR(

    54→      return srs;

    83→            fontSize: "13px",
    84→            color: "#e8601a",
    85→          }}
    86→        >

    91→      {/* Body */}
    92→      <div className="p-4 space-y-3">

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { useState } from "react";
     4→import useSWR from "swr";
     5→import Link from "next/link";
     6→import {
     7→  projects as projectsApi,
     8→  releases as releasesApi,
     9→  semanticReleases as srApi,
    10→  sources as sourcesApi,
    11→} from "@/lib/api/client";
    12→import { getProviderIcon } from "@/components/ui/provider-badge";
    13→import { timeAgo } from "@/lib/format";
    14→import { Plus } from "lucide-react";
    15→import type { Project } from "@/lib/api/types";
    16→
    17→const CHUNK_SIZE = 3;
    18→
    19→function ReleaseChips({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
    20→  const [limit, setLimit] = useState(CHUNK_SIZE);
    21→  const { data } = useSWR(
    22→    `projects/${projectId}/releases?limit=${limit + 1}`,
    23→    () => releasesApi.listByProject(projectId, 1, limit + 1)
    24→  );
    25→  const { data: sourcesData } = useSWR(
    26→    `projects/${projectId}/sources`,
    27→    () => sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)
    28→  );
    29→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
    30→  const hasMore = items.length > limit;
    31→  const shown = items.slice(0, limit);
    32→
    33→  const providerMap = new Map<string, string>();
    34→  for (const s of sourcesData?.data ?? []) {
    35→    providerMap.set(s.id, s.provider);
    36→  }
    37→
    38→  if (!data) return <span className="text-[12px] text-[#c4c4c0] italic">loading…</span>;
    39→  if (shown.length === 0) return <span className="text-[12px] text-[#c4c4c0]">—</span>;
    40→
    41→  return (
    42→    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1">
    43→      {shown.map((r) => {
    44→        const provider = providerMap.get(r.source_id);
    45→        const Icon = provider ? getProviderIcon(provider) : undefined;
    46→        return (
    47→          <Link
    48→            key={r.id}
    49→            href={`/releases/${r.id}`}
    50→            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none text-[#374151] transition-colors hover:bg-[#e8e8e5]"
    51→            style={{ backgroundColor: "#f3f3f1", fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}
    52→          >
    53→            {Icon && <Icon size={11} className="shrink-0 text-[#6b7280]" />}
    54→            {r.version}
    55→          </Link>
    56→        );
    57→      })}
    58→      {hasMore && (
    59→        <button
    60→          onClick={() => setLimit((n) => n + CHUNK_SIZE)}
    61→          className="text-[11px] text-[#e8601a] hover:underline"
    62→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
    63→        >
    64→          more…
    65→        </button>
    66→      )}
    67→    </div>
    68→  );
    69→}
    70→
    71→function SemanticReleaseChips({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
    72→  const [limit, setLimit] = useState(CHUNK_SIZE);
    73→  const { data } = useSWR(
    74→    `projects/${projectId}/sr?limit=${limit + 1}`,
    75→    () => srApi.list(projectId, 1, limit + 1)
    76→  );
    77→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
    78→  const hasMore = items.length > limit;
    79→  const shown = items.slice(0, limit);
    80→
    81→  if (!data) return <span className="text-[12px] text-[#c4c4c0] italic">loading…</span>;
    82→  if (shown.length === 0) return <span className="text-[12px] text-[#c4c4c0]">—</span>;
    83→
    84→  return (
    85→    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1">
    86→      {shown.map((sr) => (
    87→        <Link
    88→          key={sr.id}
    89→          href={`/projects/${projectId}/semantic-releases/${sr.id}`}
    90→          className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none text-[#1d4ed8] transition-colors hover:bg-[#dbeafe]"
    91→          style={{ backgroundColor: "#eff6ff", fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}
    92→        >
    93→          {sr.version}
    94→        </Link>
    95→      ))}
    96→      {hasMore && (
    97→        <button
    98→          onClick={() => setLimit((n) => n + CHUNK_SIZE)}
    99→          className="text-[11px] text-[#e8601a] hover:underline"
   100→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   101→        >
   102→          more…
   103→        </button>
   104→      )}
   105→    </div>
   106→  );
   107→}
   108→
   109→function ProjectRow({ project, index }: { project: Project; index: number }) {
   110→  return (
   111→    <tr
   112→      className="transition-colors duration-100 hover:bg-[#fafaf9]"
   113→      style={index > 0 ? { borderTop: "1px solid #e8e8e5" } : undefined}
   114→    >
   115→      <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
   116→        <Link
   117→          href={`/projects/${project.id}`}
   118→          className="font-medium text-[#e8601a] hover:underline"
   119→        >
   120→          {project.name}
   121→        </Link>
   122→      </td>
   123→      <td className="max-w-[220px] truncate px-4 py-2.5 text-[#6b7280]">
   124→        {project.description || "—"}
   125→      </td>
   126→      <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
   127→        <ReleaseChips projectId={project.id} />
   128→      </td>
   129→      <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
   130→        <SemanticReleaseChips projectId={project.id} />
   131→      </td>
   132→      <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-[12px] text-[#9ca3af]">
   133→        {timeAgo(project.created_at)}
   134→      </td>
   135→    </tr>
   136→  );
   137→}
   138→
   139→export default function ProjectsPage() {
   140→  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR("projects", () => projectsApi.list());
   141→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
   142→
   143→  return (
   144→    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 fade-in">
   145→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   146→        <div>
   147→          <h1
   148→            className="text-[24px] font-semibold text-[#111113]"
   149→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   150→          >
   151→            Projects
   152→          </h1>
   153→          <p
   154→            className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-[#6b7280]"
   155→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   156→          >
   157→            Tracked software projects and their recent releases.
   158→          </p>
   159→        </div>
   160→        <Link
   161→          href="/projects/new"
   162→          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   163→          style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   164→        >
   165→          <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   166→          New Project
   167→        </Link>
   168→      </div>
   169→
   170→      <div
   171→        className="overflow-hidden rounded-md"
   172→        style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   173→      >
   174→        {isLoading ? (
   175→          <p
   176→            className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   177→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   178→          >
   179→            Loading…
   180→          </p>
   181→        ) : items.length === 0 ? (
   182→          <p
   183→            className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   184→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   185→          >
   186→            No projects yet — create one to start tracking releases
   187→          </p>
   188→        ) : (
   189→          <table className="w-full text-[13px]" style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}>
   190→            <thead>
   191→              <tr style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
   192→                {["Name", "Description", "Recent Releases", "Semantic Releases", "Created"].map(
   193→                  (h) => (
   194→                    <th
   195→                      key={h}
   196→                      className="px-4 py-2.5 text-left text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-[#9ca3af]"
   197→                    >
   198→                      {h}
   199→                    </th>
   200→                  )
   201→                )}
   202→              </tr>
   203→            </thead>
   204→            <tbody>
   205→              {items.map((project, i) => (
   206→                <ProjectRow key={project.id} project={project} index={i} />
   207→              ))}
   208→            </tbody>
   209→          </table>
   210→        )}
   211→      </div>
   212→    </div>
   213→  );
   214→}

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { useState } from "react";
     4→import useSWR from "swr";
     5→import Link from "next/link";
     6→import {
     7→  releases as releasesApi,
     8→  projects as projectsApi,
     9→  sources as sourcesApi,
    10→} from "@/lib/api/client";
    11→import { ProviderBadge } from "@/components/ui/provider-badge";
    12→import { VersionChip } from "@/components/ui/version-chip";
    13→import type { Release, Source, Project } from "@/lib/api/types";
    14→import { ExternalLink } from "lucide-react";
    15→
    16→import { timeAgo } from "@/lib/format";
    17→
    18→interface ReleaseRow extends Release {
    19→  _projectName?: string;
    20→  _projectId?: string;
    21→  _provider?: string;
    22→  _repository?: string;
    23→}
    24→
    25→const PER_PAGE = 15;
    26→
    27→function getProviderUrl(
    28→  provider: string,
    29→  repository: string,
    30→  version: string
    31→): string | null {
    32→  switch (provider) {
    33→    case "github":
    34→      return `https://github.com/${repository}/releases/tag/${version}`;
    35→    case "dockerhub":
    36→      return `https://hub.docker.com/r/${repository}/tags?name=${encodeURIComponent(version)}`;
    37→    default:
    38→      return null;
    39→  }
    40→}
    41→
    42→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    43→/*  Page                                                               */
    44→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    45→
    46→export default function ReleasesPage() {
    47→  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
    48→  const [projectFilter, setProjectFilter] = useState<string>("all");
    49→
    50→  /* Fetch projects for the filter dropdown + source enrichment */
    51→  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR("projects-for-filter", () =>
    52→    projectsApi.list()
    53→  );
    54→
    55→  /* Build a map: sourceId -> { provider, repository, projectName, projectId } */
    56→  const { data: sourceMap } = useSWR(
    57→    projectsData ? "source-map-for-releases" : null,
    58→    async () => {
    59→      if (!projectsData?.data?.length) return new Map<string, { provider: string; repository: string; projectName: string; projectId: string }>();
    60→      const map = new Map<string, { provider: string; repository: string; projectName: string; projectId: string }>();
    61→      await Promise.all(
    62→        projectsData.data.map(async (p: Project) => {
    63→          const res = await sourcesApi.listByProject(p.id).catch(() => null);
    64→          if (res?.data) {
    65→            for (const s of res.data) {
    66→              map.set(s.id, {
    67→                provider: s.provider,
    68→                repository: s.repository,
    69→                projectName: p.name,
    70→                projectId: p.id,
    71→              });
    72→            }
    73→          }
    74→        })
    75→      );
    76→      return map;
    77→    }
    78→  );
    79→
    80→  /* Fetch releases — scoped by project or aggregated across all */
    81→  const { data: scopedData, isLoading: scopedLoading } = useSWR(
    82→    projectFilter !== "all" ? ["releases", page, projectFilter] : null,
    83→    () => releasesApi.listByProject(projectFilter, page)
    84→  );
    85→
    86→  const { data: allReleasesData, isLoading: allLoading } = useSWR(
    87→    projectFilter === "all" && projectsData
    88→      ? ["all-releases", page]
    89→      : null,
    90→    async () => {
    91→      if (!projectsData?.data?.length) return [];
    92→      const results = await Promise.all(
    93→        projectsData.data.map((p: Project) =>
    94→          releasesApi.listByProject(p.id, page).catch(() => null)
    95→        )
    96→      );
    97→      return results
    98→        .filter((r): r is NonNullable<typeof r> => r !== null)
    99→        .flatMap((r) => r.data)
   100→        .sort(
   101→          (a, b) =>
   102→            new Date(b.created_at).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at).getTime()
   103→        );
   104→    }
   105→  );
   106→
   107→  const isLoading = projectFilter !== "all" ? scopedLoading : allLoading;
   108→
   109→  /* Enrich releases with source metadata */
   110→  const rawReleases: Release[] =
   111→    projectFilter !== "all"
   112→      ? scopedData?.data ?? []
   113→      : allReleasesData ?? [];
   114→
   115→  const releases: ReleaseRow[] = rawReleases.map((r) => {
   116→    const info = sourceMap?.get(r.source_id);
   117→    return {
   118→      ...r,
   119→      _projectName: info?.projectName,
   120→      _projectId: info?.projectId,
   121→      _provider: info?.provider,
   122→      _repository: info?.repository,
   123→    };
   124→  });
   125→
   126→  /* Pagination math */
   127→  const total = projectFilter !== "all" ? (scopedData?.meta?.total ?? 0) : releases.length;
   128→  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / PER_PAGE));
   129→  const startRow = (page - 1) * PER_PAGE + 1;
   130→  const endRow = Math.min(page * PER_PAGE, total);
   131→
   132→  return (
   133→    <div className="space-y-6">
   134→      {/* Page title */}
   135→      <h1
   136→        style={{
   137→          fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   138→          fontSize: "24px",
   139→          fontWeight: 700,
   140→          color: "#111113",
   141→        }}
   142→      >
   143→        Releases
   144→      </h1>
   145→
   146→      {/* Project filter */}
   147→      <div>
   148→        <select
   149→          value={projectFilter}
   150→          onChange={(e) => {
   151→            setProjectFilter(e.target.value);
   152→            setPage(1);
   153→          }}
   154→          className="appearance-none rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-2 pr-8 outline-none transition-shadow"
   155→          style={{
   156→            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   157→            fontSize: "13px",
   158→            color: "#111113",
   159→            border: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   160→          }}
   161→          onFocus={(e) =>
   162→            (e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = "0 0 0 2px #e8601a40")
   163→          }
   164→          onBlur={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = "none")}
   165→        >
   166→          <option value="all">All Projects</option>
   167→          {projectsData?.data.map((p: Project) => (
   168→            <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
   169→              {p.name}
   170→            </option>
   171→          ))}
   172→        </select>
   173→      </div>
   174→
   175→      {/* Table card */}
   176→      <div
   177→        className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-white"
   178→        style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
   179→      >
   180→        {isLoading ? (
   181→          <div
   182→            className="py-16 text-center"
   183→            style={{
   184→              fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   185→              fontSize: "13px",
   186→              color: "#6b7280",
   187→            }}
   188→          >
   189→            Loading...
   190→          </div>
   191→        ) : releases.length === 0 ? (
   192→          <div className="py-16 text-center">
   193→            <p
   194→              style={{
   195→                fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   196→                fontStyle: "italic",
   197→                fontSize: "15px",
   198→                color: "#9ca3af",
   199→              }}
   200→            >
   201→              No releases ingested yet
   202→            </p>
   203→          </div>
   204→        ) : (
   205→          <table className="w-full">
   206→            <thead>
   207→              <tr style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
   208→                {["Project", "Provider", "Repository", "Version", "Released", "Age", ""].map(
   209→                  (col) => (
   210→                    <th
   211→                      key={col}
   212→                      className="px-4 py-3 text-left"
   213→                      style={{
   214→                        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   215→                        fontSize: "11px",
   216→                        fontWeight: 600,
   217→                        textTransform: "uppercase" as const,
   218→                        letterSpacing: "0.08em",
   219→                        color: "#9ca3af",
   220→                      }}
   221→                    >
   222→                      {col}
   223→                    </th>
   224→                  )
   225→                )}
   226→              </tr>
   227→            </thead>
   228→            <tbody>
   229→              {releases.map((release) => (
   230→                <tr
   231→                  key={release.id}
   232→                  className="transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9]"
   233→                  style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
   234→                >
   235→                  {/* Project */}
   236→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   237→                    {release._projectId ? (
   238→                      <Link
   239→                        href={`/projects/${release._projectId}`}
   240→                        className="hover:underline"
   241→                        style={{
   242→                          fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   243→                          fontSize: "13px",
   244→                          color: "#111113",
   245→                          fontWeight: 500,
   246→                        }}
   247→                      >
   248→                        {release._projectName ?? "\u2014"}
   249→                      </Link>
   250→                    ) : (
   251→                      <span
   252→                        style={{
   253→                          fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   254→                          fontSize: "13px",
   255→                          color: "#9ca3af",
   256→                        }}
   257→                      >
   258→                        {"\u2014"}
   259→                      </span>
   260→                    )}
   261→                  </td>
   262→
   263→                  {/* Provider */}
   264→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   265→                    {release._provider ? (
   266→                      <ProviderBadge provider={release._provider} />
   267→                    ) : (
   268→                      <span
   269→                        style={{
   270→                          fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   271→                          fontSize: "13px",
   272→                          color: "#9ca3af",
   273→                        }}
   274→                      >
   275→                        {"\u2014"}
   276→                      </span>
   277→                    )}
   278→                  </td>
   279→
   280→                  {/* Repository */}
   281→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   282→                    <span
   283→                      style={{
   284→                        fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   285→                        fontSize: "12px",
   286→                        color: "#374151",
   287→                      }}
   288→                    >
   289→                      {release._repository ?? release.source_id}
   290→                    </span>
   291→                  </td>
   292→
   293→                  {/* Version */}
   294→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   295→                    <Link href={`/releases/${release.id}`}>
   296→                      <VersionChip version={release.version} />
   297→                    </Link>
   298→                  </td>
   299→
   300→                  {/* Released date */}
   301→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   302→                    <span
   303→                      style={{
   304→                        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   305→                        fontSize: "13px",
   306→                        color: "#6b7280",
   307→                      }}
   308→                    >
   309→                      {release.released_at
   310→                        ? new Date(release.released_at).toLocaleDateString()
   311→                        : "\u2014"}
   312→                    </span>
   313→                  </td>
   314→
   315→                  {/* Age */}
   316→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   317→                    <span
   318→                      style={{
   319→                        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   320→                        fontSize: "13px",
   321→                        color: "#9ca3af",
   322→                      }}
   323→                    >
   324→                      {timeAgo(release.released_at ?? release.created_at)}
   325→                    </span>
   326→                  </td>
   327→
   328→                  {/* Provider link */}
   329→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   330→                    {release._provider && release._repository && (() => {
   331→                      const url = getProviderUrl(release._provider, release._repository, release.version);
   332→                      return url ? (
   333→                        <a
   334→                          href={url}
   335→                          target="_blank"
   336→                          rel="noopener noreferrer"
   337→                          className="inline-flex items-center transition-colors hover:opacity-70"
   338→                          style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}
   339→                          title="View on provider"
   340→                        >
   341→                          <ExternalLink size={14} />
   342→                        </a>
   343→                      ) : null;
   344→                    })()}
   345→                  </td>
   346→                </tr>
   347→              ))}
   348→            </tbody>
   349→          </table>
   350→        )}
   351→      </div>
   352→
   353→      {/* Pagination */}
   354→      {total > 0 && (
   355→        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   356→          <span
   357→            style={{
   358→              fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   359→              fontSize: "13px",
   360→              color: "#9ca3af",
   361→            }}
   362→          >
   363→            {startRow}&ndash;{endRow} of {total}
   364→          </span>
   365→          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   366→            <button
   367→              disabled={page <= 1}
   368→              onClick={() => setPage(page - 1)}
   369→              className="rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
   370→              style={{
   371→                fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   372→                fontSize: "13px",
   373→                color: "#374151",
   374→                border: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   375→              }}
   376→            >
   377→              Previous
   378→            </button>
   379→            <button
   380→              disabled={page >= totalPages}
   381→              onClick={() => setPage(page + 1)}
   382→              className="rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
   383→              style={{
   384→                fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   385→                fontSize: "13px",
   386→                color: "#374151",
   387→                border: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   388→              }}
   389→            >
   390→              Next
   391→            </button>
   392→          </div>
   393→        </div>
   394→      )}
   395→    </div>
   396→  );
   397→}

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import useSWR, { mutate } from "swr";
     4→import Link from "next/link";
     5→import { channels as channelsApi } from "@/lib/api/client";
     6→import { Plus, Pencil, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
     7→import { FaSlack, FaDiscord } from "react-icons/fa";
     8→import { TbWebhook } from "react-icons/tb";
     9→import type { IconType } from "react-icons";
    10→
    11→const TYPE_STYLES: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string; icon: IconType }> = {
    12→  slack: { bg: "#4A154B", text: "#ffffff", icon: FaSlack },
    13→  discord: { bg: "#5865F2", text: "#ffffff", icon: FaDiscord },
    14→  webhook: { bg: "#1a1a1a", text: "#ffffff", icon: TbWebhook },
    15→};
    16→
    17→function TypeBadge({ type }: { type: string }) {
    18→  const style = TYPE_STYLES[type.toLowerCase()];
    19→  const colors = style ?? { bg: "#6b7280", text: "#ffffff" };
    20→  const Icon = style?.icon;
    21→  return (
    22→    <span
    23→      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5"
    24→      style={{
    25→        backgroundColor: colors.bg,
    26→        color: colors.text,
    27→        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
    28→        fontSize: "12px",
    29→        fontWeight: 500,
    30→        lineHeight: "16px",
    31→      }}
    32→    >
    33→      {Icon && <Icon size={12} />}
    34→      {type}
    35→    </span>
    36→  );
    37→}
    38→
    39→export default function ChannelsPage() {
    40→  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR("channels", () => channelsApi.list());
    41→
    42→  const handleDelete = async (id: string) => {
    43→    if (!confirm("Delete this channel? This cannot be undone.")) return;
    44→    await channelsApi.delete(id);
    45→    mutate("channels");
    46→  };
    47→
    48→  const channels = data?.data ?? [];
    49→
    50→  return (
    51→    <div className="space-y-6">
    52→      {/* Header */}
    53→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
    54→        <h1
    55→          style={{
    56→            fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
    57→            fontSize: "24px",
    58→            fontWeight: 700,
    59→            color: "#111113",
    60→          }}
    61→        >
    62→          Channels
    63→        </h1>

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import useSWR from "swr";
     4→import Link from "next/link";
     5→import { projects as projectsApi, sources as sourcesApi } from "@/lib/api/client";
     6→import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
     7→import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
     8→import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from "@/components/ui/table";
     9→import type { Source } from "@/lib/api/types";
    10→
    11→interface SourceWithProject extends Source {
    12→  projectName?: string;
    13→}
    14→
    15→export default function SourcesPage() {
    16→  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR("projects-for-sources", () => projectsApi.list());
    17→
    18→  const { data: allSources, isLoading } = useSWR(
    19→    projectsData ? "all-sources" : null,
    20→    async () => {
    21→      if (!projectsData?.data?.length) return [];
    22→      const results = await Promise.all(
    23→        projectsData.data.map(async (p) => {
    24→          const res = await sourcesApi.listByProject(p.id).catch(() => null);
    25→          return (res?.data ?? []).map((s) => ({ ...s, projectName: p.name }));
    26→        })
    27→      );
    28→      return results.flat() as SourceWithProject[];
    29→    }
    30→  );
    31→
    32→  return (
    33→    <div className="space-y-4">
    34→      <div>
    35→        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">All Sources</h2>
    36→        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
    37→          Ingestion sources across all projects. Sources are managed within their project.
    38→        </p>
    39→      </div>
    40→      <Card>
    41→        <CardContent className="p-0">
    42→          {isLoading ? (
    43→            <div className="py-12 text-center text-muted-foreground">Loading...</div>
    44→          ) : (
    45→            <Table>
    46→              <TableHeader>
    47→                <TableRow>
    48→                  <TableHead>Repository</TableHead>
    49→                  <TableHead>Provider</TableHead>
    50→                  <TableHead>Project</TableHead>
    51→                  <TableHead>Poll Interval</TableHead>
    52→                  <TableHead>Last Polled</TableHead>
    53→                  <TableHead>Status</TableHead>
    54→                </TableRow>
    55→              </TableHeader>
    56→              <TableBody>
    57→                {(allSources ?? []).map((source) => (
    58→                  <TableRow key={source.id}>
    59→                    <TableCell>
    60→                      <Link href={`/sources/${source.id}/edit`} className="font-mono text-sm text-primary hover:underline">{source.repository}</Link>
    61→                    </TableCell>
    62→                    <TableCell><Badge variant="outline">{source.provider}</Badge></TableCell>
    63→                    <TableCell>
    64→                      <Link href={`/projects/${source.project_id}`} className="text-primary hover:underline">
    65→                        {source.projectName ?? source.project_id}
    66→                      </Link>
    67→                    </TableCell>
    68→                    <TableCell className="text-sm">{source.poll_interval_seconds}s</TableCell>
    69→                    <TableCell className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{source.last_polled_at ? new Date(source.last_polled_at).toLocaleString() : "Never"}</TableCell>
    70→                    <TableCell>
    71→                      {source.last_error ? (
    72→                        <Badge variant="destructive" className="text-xs">{source.last_error}</Badge>
    73→                      ) : (
    74→                        <Badge variant={source.enabled ? "default" : "secondary"}>{source.enabled ? "active" : "disabled"}</Badge>
    75→                      )}
    76→                    </TableCell>
    77→                  </TableRow>
    78→                ))}
    79→              </TableBody>
    80→            </Table>
    81→          )}
    82→        </CardContent>
    83→      </Card>
    84→    </div>
    85→  );
    86→}
    87→

Now I have the full picture. The fix involves two things:

1. **Duplicated headers**: The global `<Header>` breadcrumb shows "Projects", "Releases", etc., and then each page also has its own `<h1>` with the same title. The solution is to merge them — move the page-level action buttons (like "New Project") into the global header, and remove the duplicate `<h1>` from each page.

The simpler approach: make the global `<Header>` accept the page title & actions from each page, or just remove the page-level `<h1>` titles since the breadcrumb header already shows the page name.

Actually, the cleanest fix is the opposite: **remove the global breadcrumb header** and keep the richer page-level headers (which have descriptions and action buttons). Then **add a header to the Dashboard page** which is currently missing one.

Let me implement this.

> AGENT

Build succeeds. Here's a summary of the changes:

**Problem**: The global `<Header>` breadcrumb component in the root layout displayed the page name (e.g., "Projects", "Releases"), and each page also rendered its own `<h1>` with the same title — causing duplication. The Dashboard page had no header at all.

**Fix**:
1. **Removed the global `<Header>` breadcrumb** from `web/app/layout.tsx` — the page-level headers are richer (they include descriptions and action buttons like "New Project"), so they're the ones worth keeping.
2. **Added a "Dashboard" header** to `web/app/page.tsx` to match the style of other pages.
3. **Aligned the Sources page header** (`web/app/sources/page.tsx`) to use the same `<h1>` style as other pages instead of `<h2>`.

```
Excellent! Now I have all the information needed. Let me create a comprehensive summary report of my findings:

## Release Sorting & Fetching Analysis Report

Based on my complete search of both the frontend and backend codebases, here's a comprehensive summary of how releases are sorted and fetched:

---

### **BACKEND API - Default Sort Order**

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/pgstore.go`

All backend queries use **`ORDER BY created_at DESC`** (newest first):

1. **ListReleasesBySource** (line 191-214)
   ```go
   FROM releases WHERE source_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT $2 OFFSET $3
   ```

2. **ListReleasesByProject** (line 216-242)
   ```go
   FROM releases r JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id
   WHERE s.project_id = $1 ORDER BY r.created_at DESC LIMIT $2 OFFSET $3
   ```

3. **ListSemanticReleases** (line 461-486)
   ```go
   FROM semantic_releases WHERE project_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT $2 OFFSET $3
   ```

4. **GetSemanticReleaseSources** (line 502-521)
   ```go
   FROM releases r JOIN semantic_release_sources srs ON srs.release_id = r.id
   WHERE srs.semantic_release_id = $1 ORDER BY r.created_at DESC
   ```

5. **GetPreviousRelease** (line 621-637)
   ```go
   WHERE source_id = $1 AND version != $2 ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1
   ```

6. **ListSourcesByProject** (line 117-143)
   ```go
   FROM sources WHERE project_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT $2 OFFSET $3
   ```

---

### **API HANDLERS**

**Files:** 
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/releases.go`
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/semantic_releases.go`

The handlers simply delegate to the store methods and return paginated results. The API endpoints are:
- `GET /sources/{id}/releases` - ListBySource
- `GET /projects/{projectId}/releases` - ListByProject  
- `GET /releases/{id}` - Get single release
- `GET /projects/{projectId}/semantic-releases` - ListSemanticReleases
- `GET /semantic-releases/{id}` - Get single semantic release

---

### **FRONTEND SORTING & FETCHING**

#### **1. Releases Page** (`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/releases/page.tsx`)

**Behavior:**
- Displays releases with **client-side sorting** applied when "All Projects" filter is selected
- Line 100-103: Sorts results by `created_at` DESC:
  ```typescript
  .sort(
    (a, b) =>
      new Date(b.created_at).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at).getTime()
  )
  ```
- When a specific project is selected, relies on backend ordering

**API Calls:**
- `releasesApi.listByProject(projectId, page, perPage)` - per-project releases
- Concurrently fetches from all projects when "All Projects" is selected, then **re-sorts client-side**

---

### **API CLIENT Functions** (`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/client.ts`)

**Release endpoints:**
- Line 80-82: `releases.listBySource(sourceId, page)`

  ```

**Semantic Release endpoints:**

### **Summary Table**

| Component | Location | Sort Applied | Sort Order | Method |
|-----------|----------|--------------|-----------|--------|
| Releases Page | `/app/releases/page.tsx` | Yes (all projects) | created_at DESC | Client-side |
| Releases Page | `/app/releases/page.tsx` | No (per-project) | created_at DESC | Backend |
| Recent Releases | `dashboard/recent-releases.tsx` | Yes | created_at DESC | Client-side |
| ReleaseChips | `projects/page.tsx` | No | created_at DESC | Backend |
| SemanticReleaseChips | `projects/page.tsx` | No | created_at DESC | Backend |
| Semantic Releases List | `semantic-releases/semantic-releases-list.tsx` | No | created_at DESC | Backend |
| Project Detail SR Tab | `project-detail.tsx` | No | created_at DESC | Backend |

---

### **Key Findings**

1. **Backend uses consistent ordering:** All queries sort by `created_at DESC` (newest releases first)
2. **Frontend applies additional sorting:** Only when aggregating results from multiple projects (Releases page "All Projects", Recent Releases dashboard)
3. **Components use backend ordering:** Most components rely solely on backend-provided order
4. **No version-based sorting:** Sorting is purely time-based (`created_at`), not semantic version ordering
5. **Pagination defaults:** Most endpoints use `page=1` with varying `per_page` values (default 25 for releases, 15 for releases page display)

   189→// --- ReleasesStore ---
   190→
   191→func (s *PgStore) ListReleasesBySource(ctx context.Context, sourceID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error) {
   192→	var total int
   193→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM releases WHERE source_id = $1`, sourceID).Scan(&total)
   194→	if err != nil {
   195→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("count releases: %w", err)
   196→	}
   197→	offset := (page - 1) * perPage
   198→	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
   199→		`SELECT id, source_id, version, COALESCE(raw_data,'{}'), released_at, created_at
   200→		 FROM releases WHERE source_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT $2 OFFSET $3`, sourceID, perPage, offset)
   201→	if err != nil {
   202→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("list releases by source: %w", err)
   203→	}
   204→	defer rows.Close()
   205→	var releases []models.Release
   206→	for rows.Next() {
   207→		var rel models.Release
   208→		if err := rows.Scan(&rel.ID, &rel.SourceID, &rel.Version, &rel.RawData, &rel.ReleasedAt, &rel.CreatedAt); err != nil {
   209→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("scan release: %w", err)
   210→		}
   211→		releases = append(releases, rel)
   212→	}
   213→	return releases, total, nil
   214→}
   215→
   216→func (s *PgStore) ListReleasesByProject(ctx context.Context, projectID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error) {
   217→	var total int
   218→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   219→		`SELECT COUNT(*) FROM releases r JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id WHERE s.project_id = $1`,
   220→		projectID).Scan(&total)
   221→	if err != nil {
   222→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("count releases: %w", err)
   223→	}
   224→	offset := (page - 1) * perPage
   225→	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
   226→		`SELECT r.id, r.source_id, r.version, COALESCE(r.raw_data,'{}'), r.released_at, r.created_at
   227→		 FROM releases r JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id
   228→		 WHERE s.project_id = $1 ORDER BY r.created_at DESC LIMIT $2 OFFSET $3`, projectID, perPage, offset)
   229→	if err != nil {
   230→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("list releases by project: %w", err)
   231→	}
   232→	defer rows.Close()
   233→	var releases []models.Release
   234→	for rows.Next() {
   235→		var rel models.Release
   236→		if err := rows.Scan(&rel.ID, &rel.SourceID, &rel.Version, &rel.RawData, &rel.ReleasedAt, &rel.CreatedAt); err != nil {
   237→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("scan release: %w", err)
   238→		}
   239→		releases = append(releases, rel)
   240→	}
   241→	return releases, total, nil
   242→}

     2→"use client";

     4→import useSWR from "swr";

     6→import {
     7→  projects as projectsApi,

    15→
    16→interface ReleaseRow extends Release {
    17→  _repository?: string;
    18→  _provider?: string;
    19→}
    20→
    21→export function RecentReleases() {
    22→  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR("projects-for-dashboard", () =>
    23→    projectsApi.list()
    24→  );
    25→
    26→  const { data: allReleases, isLoading } = useSWR(
    27→    projectsData ? "recent-releases" : null,
    28→    async () => {
    29→      if (!projectsData?.data?.length) return [];
    30→
    31→      // Fetch releases and sources in parallel per project
    32→      const projectSlice = projectsData.data.slice(0, 10);
    33→      const [releaseResults, sourceResults] = await Promise.all([
    34→        Promise.all(
    35→          projectSlice.map((p) =>
    36→            releasesApi.listByProject(p.id, 1).catch(() => null)
    37→          )
    38→        ),
    39→        Promise.all(
    40→          projectSlice.map((p) =>
    41→            sourcesApi.listByProject(p.id, 1).catch(() => null)
    42→          )
    43→        ),
    44→      ]);
    45→
    46→      // Build source_id -> repository and source_id -> provider maps
    47→      const sourceMap = new Map<string, string>();
    48→      const providerMap = new Map<string, string>();
    49→      sourceResults
    50→        .filter((r): r is NonNullable<typeof r> => r !== null)
    51→        .flatMap((r) => r.data)
    52→        .forEach((s: Source) => {
    53→          sourceMap.set(s.id, s.repository);
    54→          providerMap.set(s.id, s.provider);
    55→        });
    56→
    57→      const releases: ReleaseRow[] = releaseResults
    58→        .filter((r): r is NonNullable<typeof r> => r !== null)
    59→        .flatMap((r) => r.data)
    60→        .sort(
    61→          (a, b) =>
    62→            new Date(b.created_at).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at).getTime()
    63→        )
    64→        .slice(0, 8)
    65→        .map((rel) => ({
    66→          ...rel,
    67→          _repository: sourceMap.get(rel.source_id),
    68→          _provider: providerMap.get(rel.source_id),
    69→        }));
    70→
    71→      return releases;
    72→    }
    73→  );
    74→
    75→  return (
    76→    <div
    77→      className="rounded-lg bg-white"
    78→      style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
    79→    >
    80→      {/* Header */}
    81→      <div
    82→        className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-4"
    83→        style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
    84→      >
    85→        <h3
    86→          className="font-medium"
    87→          style={{
    88→            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
    89→            fontSize: "13px",
    90→            color: "#111113",
    91→          }}
    92→        >
    93→          Recent Source Releases
    94→        </h3>
    95→        <Link
    96→          href="/releases"
    97→          className="text-sm hover:underline"
    98→          style={{
    99→            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   100→            fontSize: "13px",
   101→            color: "#e8601a",
   102→          }}
   103→        >
   104→          View all &rarr;
   105→        </Link>
   106→      </div>
   107→
   108→      {/* Body */}
   109→      <div>
   110→        {isLoading ? (
   111→          <div
   112→            className="py-12 text-center"
   113→            style={{
   114→              fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   115→              fontSize: "13px",
   116→              color: "#6b7280",
   117→            }}
   118→          >
   119→            Loading...
   120→          </div>
   121→        ) : allReleases && allReleases.length > 0 ? (
   122→          allReleases.map((release: ReleaseRow, idx: number) => {
   123→            const Icon = release._provider ? getProviderIcon(release._provider) : undefined;
   124→            return (
   125→            <Link
   126→              key={release.id}
   127→              href={`/releases/${release.id}`}
   128→              className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-3 transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9]"
   129→              style={
   130→                idx < allReleases.length - 1
   131→                  ? { borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }
   132→                  : undefined
   133→              }
   134→            >
   135→              <span
   136→                className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-1.5 truncate"
   137→                style={{
   138→                  fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   139→                  fontSize: "13px",
   140→                  color: "#6b7280",
   141→                }}
   142→              >
   143→                {Icon && <Icon size={13} className="shrink-0 text-[#9ca3af]" />}
   144→                {release._repository ?? release.source_id.slice(0, 12)}
   145→              </span>
   146→              <div className="ml-3 flex items-center gap-3">
   147→                <VersionChip version={release.version} />
   148→                <span
   149→                  className="whitespace-nowrap"
   150→                  style={{
   151→                    fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   152→                    fontSize: "12px",
   153→                    color: "#9ca3af",
   154→                  }}
   155→                >
   156→                  {timeAgo(release.released_at ?? release.created_at)}
   157→                </span>
   158→              </div>
   159→            </Link>
   160→          );})
   161→        ) : (

   163→            <p
   164→              style={{
   165→                fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   166→                fontStyle: "italic",
   167→                fontSize: "14px",
   168→                color: "#9ca3af",
   169→              }}
   170→            >
   171→              No releases yet
   172→            </p>
   173→          </div>
   174→        )}
   175→      </div>
   176→    </div>
   177→  );
   178→}

   502→func (s *PgStore) GetSemanticReleaseSources(ctx context.Context, id string) ([]models.Release, error) {
   503→	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
   504→		`SELECT r.id, r.source_id, r.version, COALESCE(r.raw_data,'{}'), r.released_at, r.created_at
   505→		 FROM releases r
   506→		 JOIN semantic_release_sources srs ON srs.release_id = r.id
   507→		 WHERE srs.semantic_release_id = $1 ORDER BY r.created_at DESC`, id)

   516→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("scan release: %w", err)
   517→		}
   518→		releases = append(releases, rel)
   519→	}
   520→	return releases, nil
   521→}

   619→// the given version, ordered by created_at descending. Returns nil (no error)

   621→func (s *PgStore) GetPreviousRelease(ctx context.Context, sourceID string, beforeVersion string) (*models.Release, error) {
   622→	var rel models.Release
   623→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   624→		`SELECT id, source_id, version, COALESCE(raw_data,'{}'), released_at, created_at

   627→		 ORDER BY created_at DESC

   635→	}
   636→	return &rel, nil
   637→}

> AGENT

Wait — these two cases are different from the per-project filtered case. Both are aggregating results from **multiple projects** client-side (fetching page 1 from each project, then merging). The sort here is actually needed to interleave results from different projects. The backend can't do this merge since there's no single "all releases across all projects" endpoint.

But the dashboard `recent-releases.tsx` sort is still valid — it merges from multiple projects and takes top 8. The releases page "all projects" case also merges across projects.

The real issue is: since the backend now sorts by `released_at`, these client-side merges should also sort by `released_at` to stay consistent — which they already do after my last change.

So the backend fix was the substantive change. The frontend sorts are only used when merging across multiple project API calls, which is correct behavior. There's nothing wrong to fix here.

Let me confirm the per-project filtered path on the releases page has no client-side sort (it shouldn't — it relies purely on backend order).

    10→// ReleasesStore defines the persistence operations for releases (read-only).
    11→type ReleasesStore interface {
    12→	ListReleasesBySource(ctx context.Context, sourceID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error)
    13→	ListReleasesByProject(ctx context.Context, projectID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error)
    14→	GetRelease(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Release, error)
    15→}
    16→
    17→// ReleasesHandler implements HTTP handlers for the /releases resource.
    18→type ReleasesHandler struct {
    19→	store ReleasesStore
    20→}
    21→
    22→// NewReleasesHandler returns a new ReleasesHandler.
    23→func NewReleasesHandler(store ReleasesStore) *ReleasesHandler {
    24→	return &ReleasesHandler{store: store}
    25→}
    26→
    27→// ListBySource handles GET /sources/{id}/releases — returns releases for a specific source.
    28→func (h *ReleasesHandler) ListBySource(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    29→	sourceID := r.PathValue("id")
    30→	if sourceID == "" {
    31→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Source ID is required")
    32→		return
    33→	}
    34→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    35→	releases, total, err := h.store.ListReleasesBySource(r.Context(), sourceID, page, perPage)
    36→	if err != nil {
    37→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list releases")
    38→		return
    39→	}
    40→	if releases == nil {
    41→		releases = []models.Release{}
    42→	}
    43→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, releases, page, perPage, total)
    44→}
    45→
    46→// ListByProject handles GET /projects/{projectId}/releases — returns releases for all sources in a project.
    47→func (h *ReleasesHandler) ListByProject(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    48→	projectID := r.PathValue("projectId")
    49→	if projectID == "" {
    50→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Project ID is required")
    51→		return
    52→	}
    53→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    54→	releases, total, err := h.store.ListReleasesByProject(r.Context(), projectID, page, perPage)
    55→	if err != nil {
    56→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list releases")
    57→		return
    58→	}
    59→	if releases == nil {
    60→		releases = []models.Release{}
    61→	}
    62→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, releases, page, perPage, total)
    63→}
    64→
    65→// Get handles GET /releases/{id} — returns a single release.
    66→func (h *ReleasesHandler) Get(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    67→	id := r.PathValue("id")
    68→	if id == "" {
    69→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Release ID is required")
    70→		return
    71→	}
    72→	rel, err := h.store.GetRelease(r.Context(), id)
    73→	if err != nil {
    74→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Release not found")
    75→		return
    76→	}
    77→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, rel)
    78→}

    21→const BASE = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || "/api/v1";
    22→
    23→async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
    24→  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
    25→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...init?.headers },
    26→    ...init,
    27→  });
    28→  if (!res.ok) {
    29→    const body = await res.json().catch(() => null);
    30→    throw new Error(body?.error?.message ?? `Request failed: ${res.status}`);
    31→  }
    32→  return res.json();
    33→}
    34→

     1→package api
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"net/http"
     5→
     6→	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
     7→)
     8→
     9→// Dependencies holds all external dependencies required for registering API routes.
    10→type Dependencies struct {
    11→	DB                    *pgxpool.Pool
    12→	ProjectsStore         ProjectsStore
    13→	ReleasesStore         ReleasesStore
    14→	SubscriptionsStore    SubscriptionsStore
    15→	SourcesStore          SourcesStore
    16→	ChannelsStore         ChannelsStore
    17→	ContextSourcesStore   ContextSourcesStore
    18→	SemanticReleasesStore SemanticReleasesStore
    19→	AgentStore            AgentStore
    20→	KeyStore              KeyStore
    21→	HealthChecker         HealthChecker
    22→	Broadcaster           *Broadcaster
    23→	NoAuth                bool
    24→}
    25→
    26→// RegisterRoutes registers all API v1 routes on the given ServeMux.
    27→func RegisterRoutes(mux *http.ServeMux, deps Dependencies) {
    28→	// Public chain: no auth required.
    29→	publicChain := Chain(RequestID, Logger, Recovery)
    30→
    31→	// Authenticated chain: includes auth unless NoAuth is set.
    32→	chain := publicChain
    33→	if !deps.NoAuth {
    34→		chain = Chain(RequestID, Logger, Recovery, RateLimit(10, 20), Auth(deps.KeyStore))
    35→	}
    36→
    37→	// Projects (CRUD)
    38→	projects := NewProjectsHandler(deps.ProjectsStore)
    39→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.List)))
    40→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/projects", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Create)))
    41→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Get)))
    42→	mux.Handle("PUT /api/v1/projects/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Update)))
    43→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/projects/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Delete)))
    44→
    45→	// Sources (nested under projects)
    46→	sources := NewSourcesHandler(deps.SourcesStore)
    47→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/sources", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.List)))
    48→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/sources", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.Create)))
    49→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.Get)))
    50→	mux.Handle("PUT /api/v1/sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.Update)))
    51→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.Delete)))
    52→
    53→	// Context Sources (nested under projects)
    54→	contextSources := NewContextSourcesHandler(deps.ContextSourcesStore)
    55→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/context-sources", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.List)))
    56→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/context-sources", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.Create)))
    57→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/context-sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.Get)))
    58→	mux.Handle("PUT /api/v1/context-sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.Update)))
    59→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/context-sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.Delete)))
    60→
    61→	// Releases (read-only, nested under sources and projects)
    62→	releases := NewReleasesHandler(deps.ReleasesStore)
    63→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/sources/{id}/releases", chain(http.HandlerFunc(releases.ListBySource)))
    64→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/releases", chain(http.HandlerFunc(releases.ListByProject)))
    65→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/releases/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(releases.Get)))
    66→
    67→	// Semantic Releases (read-only, nested under projects)
    68→	semanticReleases := NewSemanticReleasesHandler(deps.SemanticReleasesStore)
    69→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/semantic-releases", chain(http.HandlerFunc(semanticReleases.List)))
    70→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/semantic-releases/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(semanticReleases.Get)))
    71→
    72→	// Subscriptions (CRUD)
    73→	subscriptions := NewSubscriptionsHandler(deps.SubscriptionsStore)
    74→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/subscriptions", chain(http.HandlerFunc(subscriptions.List)))
    75→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/subscriptions", chain(http.HandlerFunc(subscriptions.Create)))
    76→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/subscriptions/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(subscriptions.Get)))
    77→	mux.Handle("PUT /api/v1/subscriptions/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(subscriptions.Update)))
    78→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/subscriptions/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(subscriptions.Delete)))
    79→
    80→	// Channels (CRUD)
    81→	channels := NewChannelsHandler(deps.ChannelsStore)
    82→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/channels", chain(http.HandlerFunc(channels.List)))
    83→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/channels", chain(http.HandlerFunc(channels.Create)))
    84→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/channels/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(channels.Get)))
    85→	mux.Handle("PUT /api/v1/channels/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(channels.Update)))
    86→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/channels/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(channels.Delete)))
    87→
    88→	// Agent
    89→	agent := NewAgentHandler(deps.AgentStore)
    90→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/agent/run", chain(http.HandlerFunc(agent.TriggerRun)))
    91→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/agent/runs", chain(http.HandlerFunc(agent.ListRuns)))
    92→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/agent-runs/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(agent.GetRun)))
    93→
    94→	// Providers (metadata — static, no store needed)
    95→	providers := NewProvidersHandler()
    96→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/providers", chain(http.HandlerFunc(providers.List)))
    97→
    98→	// SSE events
    99→	events := NewEventsHandler(deps.Broadcaster)
   100→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/events", chain(http.HandlerFunc(events.Stream)))
   101→
   102→	// Health (public — no auth middleware)
   103→	health := NewHealthHandler(deps.HealthChecker)
   104→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/health", publicChain(http.HandlerFunc(health.Check)))
   105→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/stats", chain(http.HandlerFunc(health.Stats)))
   106→}

  "new_string": "// --- ReleasesStore ---\n\nfunc (s *PgStore) ListAllReleases(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error) {\n\tvar total int\n\terr := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM releases`).Scan(&total)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"count releases: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\toffset := (page - 1) * perPage\n\trows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT id, source_id, version, COALESCE(raw_data,'{}'), released_at, created_at\n\t\t FROM releases ORDER BY COALESCE(released_at, created_at) DESC LIMIT $1 OFFSET $2`, perPage, offset)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"list all releases: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\tvar releases []models.Release\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar rel models.Release\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&rel.ID, &rel.SourceID, &rel.Version, &rel.RawData, &rel.ReleasedAt, &rel.CreatedAt); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"scan release: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\treleases = append(releases, rel)\n\t}\n\treturn releases, total, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *PgStore) ListReleasesBySource(ctx context.Context, sourceID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.Release, int, error) {"
}

}

> AGENT

Pushed `fac5b58`. Here's what changed:

**Backend:**
- Added `ListAllReleases` query in `pgstore.go` — `SELECT ... FROM releases ORDER BY COALESCE(released_at, created_at) DESC` with proper `LIMIT`/`OFFSET` pagination
- Added `List` handler on `GET /api/v1/releases` 
- Updated the `ReleasesStore` interface and test mock

**Frontend:**
- Added `releases.list(page, perPage)` API client method
- Releases page now calls the single `/releases` endpoint for "All Projects" instead of fetching from each project separately — pagination and sort order are fully server-side

The problem: `releasesApi.list(page)` doesn't pass `PER_PAGE` — it defaults to 25 on the backend. But the pagination display uses `PER_PAGE = 15`. So the backend returns 25 rows while the UI says "1-15 of 35".

> AGENT

The backend model has `json:"last_polled_at,omitempty"` — when `LastPolledAt` is `nil` (never polled), the field is omitted from JSON. That's fine and the frontend handles it with `? ... : "Never"`.

But the question is: is `last_polled_at` actually being updated after polling? Let me check the ingestion code.

     1→package ingestion
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"log/slog"
     6→	"time"
     7→)
     8→
     9→// Orchestrator runs polling ingestion sources on a fixed interval.
    10→type Orchestrator struct {
    11→	service       *Service
    12→	loader        *SourceLoader
    13→	staticSources []IIngestionSource // for testing only
    14→	interval      time.Duration
    15→}
    16→
    17→// NewOrchestrator creates an orchestrator that dynamically loads enabled
    18→// sources from the database on each poll cycle.
    19→func NewOrchestrator(service *Service, loader *SourceLoader, interval time.Duration) *Orchestrator {
    20→	return &Orchestrator{service: service, loader: loader, interval: interval}
    21→}
    22→
    23→// NewOrchestratorWithSources creates an orchestrator with a static source list.
    24→// Intended for testing; production code should use NewOrchestrator with a SourceLoader.
    25→func NewOrchestratorWithSources(service *Service, sources []IIngestionSource, interval time.Duration) *Orchestrator {
    26→	return &Orchestrator{service: service, staticSources: sources, interval: interval}
    27→}
    28→
    29→// Run polls all sources immediately, then on every tick. Blocks until ctx is cancelled.
    30→func (o *Orchestrator) Run(ctx context.Context) {
    31→	ticker := time.NewTicker(o.interval)
    32→	defer ticker.Stop()
    33→
    34→	o.pollAll(ctx)
    35→
    36→	for {
    37→		select {
    38→		case <-ctx.Done():
    39→			return
    40→		case <-ticker.C:
    41→			o.pollAll(ctx)
    42→		}
    43→	}
    44→}
    45→
    46→func (o *Orchestrator) pollAll(ctx context.Context) {
    47→	sources := o.staticSources
    48→	if sources == nil {
    49→		var err error
    50→		sources, err = o.loader.LoadEnabledSources(ctx)
    51→		if err != nil {
    52→			slog.Error("load sources failed", "err", err)
    53→			return
    54→		}
    55→	}
    56→	if len(sources) == 0 {
    57→		slog.Debug("no enabled sources to poll")
    58→		return
    59→	}
    60→	for _, src := range sources {
    61→		results, err := src.FetchNewReleases(ctx)
    62→		if err != nil {
    63→			slog.Error("poll failed", "source", src.Name(), "err", err)
    64→			continue
    65→		}
    66→		if len(results) == 0 {
    67→			continue
    68→		}
    69→		if err := o.service.ProcessResults(ctx, src.SourceID(), src.Name(), results); err != nil {
    70→			slog.Error("process failed", "source", src.Name(), "err", err)
    71→		}
    72→	}
    73→}
    74→

     1→package ingestion
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→
     8→	"github.com/google/uuid"
     9→	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"
    10→	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
    11→	"github.com/riverqueue/river"
    12→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/queue"
    13→)
    14→
    15→// PgStore implements ReleaseStore using PostgreSQL + River for the transactional outbox.
    16→type PgStore struct {
    17→	pool  *pgxpool.Pool
    18→	river *river.Client[pgx.Tx]
    19→}
    20→
    21→func NewPgStore(pool *pgxpool.Pool, riverClient *river.Client[pgx.Tx]) *PgStore {
    22→	return &PgStore{pool: pool, river: riverClient}
    23→}
    24→
    25→// IngestRelease inserts a release and enqueues a notify job in a single transaction.
    26→// Returns an error on unique constraint violation (caller treats as idempotent skip).
    27→func (s *PgStore) IngestRelease(ctx context.Context, sourceID string, result *IngestionResult) error {
    28→	tx, err := s.pool.Begin(ctx)
    29→	if err != nil {
    30→		return fmt.Errorf("begin tx: %w", err)
    31→	}
    32→	defer tx.Rollback(ctx)
    33→
    34→	// Build raw_data from Metadata + Changelog so nothing is lost.
    35→	raw := make(map[string]string)
    36→	for k, v := range result.Metadata {
    37→		raw[k] = v
    38→	}
    39→	if result.Changelog != "" {
    40→		raw["changelog"] = result.Changelog
    41→	}
    42→	rawData, err := json.Marshal(raw)
    43→	if err != nil {
    44→		return fmt.Errorf("marshal raw_data: %w", err)
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	releaseID := uuid.New().String()
    48→	_, err = tx.Exec(ctx,
    49→		`INSERT INTO releases (id, source_id, version, raw_data, released_at) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)`,
    50→		releaseID, sourceID, result.RawVersion, rawData, result.Timestamp,
    51→	)
    52→	if err != nil {
    53→		return fmt.Errorf("insert release: %w", err)
    54→	}
    55→
    56→	_, err = s.river.InsertTx(ctx, tx, queue.NotifyJobArgs{
    57→		ReleaseID: releaseID,
    58→		SourceID:  sourceID,
    59→	}, nil)
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		return fmt.Errorf("enqueue job: %w", err)
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	if err := tx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {
    65→		return fmt.Errorf("commit: %w", err)
    66→	}
    67→	return nil
    68→}
    69→

     1→package ingestion
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"log/slog"
     7→	"strings"
     8→)
     9→
    10→// Service orchestrates ingestion sources and persists results.
    11→type Service struct {
    12→	store ReleaseStore
    13→}
    14→
    15→func NewService(store ReleaseStore) *Service {
    16→	return &Service{store: store}
    17→}
    18→
    19→// ProcessResults persists raw ingestion results via the store.
    20→// Duplicate releases (unique constraint violations) are logged and skipped, not fatal.
    21→func (s *Service) ProcessResults(ctx context.Context, sourceID string, sourceName string, results []IngestionResult) error {
    22→	for _, r := range results {
    23→		if err := s.store.IngestRelease(ctx, sourceID, &r); err != nil {
    24→			if isUniqueViolation(err) {
    25→				slog.Debug("duplicate release, skipping", "version", r.RawVersion)
    26→				continue
    27→			}
    28→			return fmt.Errorf("ingest %s: %w", r.RawVersion, err)
    29→		}
    30→		slog.Info("ingested release", "source", sourceName, "version", r.RawVersion)
    31→	}
    32→	return nil
    33→}
    34→
    35→// isUniqueViolation checks if the error is a PostgreSQL unique constraint violation.
    36→// pgx wraps the PG error; we check for the SQLSTATE code 23505.
    37→func isUniqueViolation(err error) bool {
    38→	return strings.Contains(err.Error(), "23505") ||
    39→		strings.Contains(err.Error(), "unique_violation")
    40→}
    41→

     1→package ingestion
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"log/slog"
     7→	"net/http"
     8→
     9→	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
    10→)
    11→
    12→// SourceLoader reads source configuration from the database and constructs
    13→// IIngestionSource instances. It bridges the API-managed sources table with
    14→// the polling orchestrator.
    15→type SourceLoader struct {
    16→	pool   *pgxpool.Pool
    17→	client *http.Client
    18→}
    19→
    20→func NewSourceLoader(pool *pgxpool.Pool, client *http.Client) *SourceLoader {
    21→	return &SourceLoader{pool: pool, client: client}
    22→}
    23→
    24→// LoadEnabledSources queries all enabled sources and constructs the
    25→// appropriate IIngestionSource for each one based on provider.
    26→func (l *SourceLoader) LoadEnabledSources(ctx context.Context) ([]IIngestionSource, error) {
    27→	rows, err := l.pool.Query(ctx,
    28→		`SELECT id, provider, repository FROM sources WHERE enabled = true`)
    29→	if err != nil {
    30→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("query enabled sources: %w", err)
    31→	}
    32→	defer rows.Close()
    33→
    34→	var sources []IIngestionSource
    35→	for rows.Next() {
    36→		var id string
    37→		var sourceType, repository string
    38→		if err := rows.Scan(&id, &sourceType, &repository); err != nil {
    39→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("scan source row: %w", err)
    40→		}
    41→
    42→		src := l.buildSource(id, sourceType, repository)
    43→		if src == nil {
    44→			slog.Warn("unsupported source type, skipping",
    45→				"id", id, "type", sourceType, "repo", repository)
    46→			continue
    47→		}
    48→		sources = append(sources, src)
    49→	}
    50→	return sources, rows.Err()
    51→}
    52→
    53→// LookupSourceID finds the source ID for a given (provider, repository) pair.
    54→// Returns empty string and false if no matching enabled source exists.
    55→func (l *SourceLoader) LookupSourceID(ctx context.Context, provider, repository string) (string, bool) {
    56→	var id string
    57→	err := l.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
    58→		`SELECT id FROM sources WHERE provider = $1 AND repository = $2 AND enabled = true`,
    59→		provider, repository,
    60→	).Scan(&id)
    61→	if err != nil {
    62→		return "", false
    63→	}
    64→	return id, true
    65→}
    66→
    67→func (l *SourceLoader) buildSource(id string, sourceType, repository string) IIngestionSource {
    68→	switch sourceType {
    69→	case "dockerhub":
    70→		return NewDockerHubSource(l.client, repository, id)
    71→	case "github":
    72→		return NewGitHubAtomSource(l.client, repository, id)
    73→	default:
    74→		return nil
    75→	}
    76→}
    77→

> AGENT

The problem is clear: `last_polled_at` is never updated anywhere. The right place is in `orchestrator.go` after polling each source — update `last_polled_at` (and `last_error` on failure). The orchestrator already has access to the `pool` via the loader. The cleanest approach is to update it directly in `pollAll`.

Let me add this to the orchestrator using the loader's pool.

}

  "new_string": "\tfor _, src := range sources {\n\t\tresults, err := src.FetchNewReleases(ctx)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tslog.Error(\"poll failed\", \"source\", src.Name(), \"err\", err)\n\t\t\to.updateSourcePollStatus(ctx, src.SourceID(), err)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\to.updateSourcePollStatus(ctx, src.SourceID(), nil)\n\t\tif len(results) == 0 {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := o.service.ProcessResults(ctx, src.SourceID(), src.Name(), results); err != nil {\n\t\t\tslog.Error(\"process failed\", \"source\", src.Name(), \"err\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (o *Orchestrator) updateSourcePollStatus(ctx context.Context, sourceID string, pollErr error) {\n\tif o.pool == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tvar lastError *string\n\tif pollErr != nil {\n\t\ts := pollErr.Error()\n\t\tlastError = &s\n\t}\n\t_, err := o.pool.Exec(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sources SET last_polled_at = NOW(), last_error = $1 WHERE id = $2`,\n\t\tlastError, sourceID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tslog.Error(\"update source poll status\", \"source\", sourceID, \"err\", err)\n\t}\n}"

> AGENT

Pushed `e1cf7bb`. The issue was that the orchestrator polled sources but never updated `last_polled_at` in the database. Now after each source poll, it runs `UPDATE sources SET last_polled_at = NOW(), last_error = $1 WHERE id = $2`, setting the timestamp and clearing/setting the error accordingly.

> DEVELOPER

    94→// Update handles PUT /sources/{id} — updates an existing source.
    95→func (h *SourcesHandler) Update(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    96→	id := r.PathValue("id")
    97→	if id == "" {
    98→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid source ID")
    99→		return
   100→	}
   101→	var src models.Source
   102→	if err := DecodeJSON(r, &src); err != nil {
   103→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid JSON body")
   104→		return
   105→	}
   106→	src.Provider = strings.TrimSpace(src.Provider)
   107→	src.Repository = strings.TrimSpace(src.Repository)
   108→	if src.Provider == "" {
   109→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "provider is required")
   110→		return
   111→	}
   112→	if src.Repository == "" {
   113→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "repository is required")
   114→		return
   115→	}
   116→	if err := h.store.UpdateSource(r.Context(), id, &src); err != nil {
   117→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Source not found")
   118→		return
   119→	}
   120→	src.ID = id
   121→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, src)
   122→}
   123→
